Abstract: Dirty entity resolution (ER), which identifies records referring to the same real-world entity from a single, messy dataset, is a fundamental task in data management and mining. However, the dominant blocking-matching-clustering paradigm for ER suffers from critical flaws. Its cascaded, decoupled workflow essentially produces a static, sparse graph plagued by missing edges (due to blocking failures) and noisy links (due to matching errors), causing error propagation and yielding suboptimal clusters, particularly when rigid transitivity is imposed in the clustering. We contend that matching and clustering are fundamentally synergistic, both optimizing for the construction of an ideal entity graph. Building upon this insight, we propose Alper, a unified framework that integrates these steps into an iterative probabilistic label propagation process over a global, evolving graph. Unlike disjoint blocking, Alper refines the graph structure and labels dynamically by adaptively integrating "weak but cheap" signals from graph propagation with "strong but expensive" LLM-based pairwise queries. For higher cost-effectiveness, we formulate the signal selection as a constrained optimization problem maximizing cumulative marginal gain under a query budget, solved via our greedy algorithm with provable theoretical guarantees. Our extensive experiments over eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that Alper is consistently superior to state-of-the-art cascaded pipelines.

</details>


### [115] [Fine-Tuning Over Architectural Complexity: Broad-Coverage PII Detection on PIIBench with DeBERTa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25816)
*Pritesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在异构文本上提升PII检测覆盖范围的方法。通过构建包含82种实体类型、横跨10个数据源的多源PIIBench数据集，比较了三种DeBERTa微调方案（直接令牌分类微调、源条件层次模型SC+H、三阶段课程扩展SC+H+Curr）。实验表明，直接微调的DeBERTa在5,000条测试子集上F1达0.6476，远超最强的已发表对比系统（0.1723）；在完整100,002条测试集上仍保持优势（F1 0.6455 vs SC+H的0.5894）。直接微调在82种细粒度类型中获胜54种，全部10个粗粒度组别均占优。结论：多样化的任务特定训练数据和简单的加权交叉熵目标比架构和课程复杂性对广覆盖PII检测贡献更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测系统通常在狭窄的源域或边界内训练，当部署在异构文本上时覆盖范围受限。本文旨在通过多源数据融合和模型微调策略来提升PII检测的广覆盖能力。

Method: 1) 构建多源PIIBench数据集，包含82种实体类型，横跨10个源数据集；2) 评估三种基于DeBERTa的方法：直接令牌分类微调、源条件层次模型（SC+H）、三阶段课程扩展（SC+H+Curr）；3) 在5,000条可复现测试子集和完整100,002条测试集上进行流式评估。

Result: 直接微调DeBERTa在5,000条子集上F1为0.6476，SC+H为0.5899，SC+H+Curr为0.2772，最强已发表对比系统仅0.1723。完整测试集上直接微调F1为0.6455，SC+H为0.5894。直接微调在82种实体类型中获胜54种，全部10个粗粒度组别均占优。

Conclusion: 多样化的任务特定训练数据和简单的加权交叉熵目标比复杂的架构设计和课程学习策略对广覆盖PII检测贡献更大，直接微调是当前最优方案。

Abstract: Personally identifiable information (PII) detection systems are frequently trained within narrow source or domain boundaries, limiting coverage when deployed on heterogeneous text. We study model fine-tuning on a corrected multi-source PIIBench preparation spanning 82 retained entity types across ten source datasets. We evaluate three DeBERTa-based approaches: direct token classification fine-tuning, a source-conditioned hierarchical model (SC+H), and a three-phase curriculum extension (SC+H+Curr). Against eight published comparator systems on a reproducible 5,000-record held-out subset (test_5k), direct fine-tuned DeBERTa achieves F1 0.6476, while SC+H and the curriculum variant achieve 0.5899 and 0.2772 respectively; the strongest published comparator reaches only 0.1723. Because validation initially favoured SC+H, we perform a final streamed evaluation on the complete 100,002-record held-out split. Direct fine-tuning remains superior, achieving F1 0.6455 versus 0.5894 for SC+H. Entity-level analysis shows that direct fine tuning wins 54 of 82 fine entity types and all ten coarse groups by support-weighted entity F1, while SC+H retains localised advantages on 28 types. The results indicate that diverse task-specific training data and a simple weighted cross-entropy objective contribute more to broad-coverage PII detection than the tested architectural and curriculum complexity.

</details>


### [116] [Clarify, Abstain or Answer? Strategising in Conversation with Belief-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25831)
*Joris Baan,Wilker Aziz,Barbara Plank,Raquel Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出信念增强生成（BAG）方法，通过让LLM基于自身采样K个回复形成的信念状态，利用提示引导模型推理并决定回答、澄清或回避的对话策略。在多种模型上，BAG相比纯提示基线提升了多轮模糊QA的准确率，并使策略决策更忠实于信念状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅将LLM的文本分布用于解码或选择性预测等狭窄任务，且常需人工干预，无法直接控制生成。作者希望利用模型自身的信念状态，让模型自主推理并决定对话策略（回答、澄清或回避）。

Method: BAG通过让LLM采样K个响应形成信念状态，将这一信念状态通过提示注入模型，引导模型对K个样本进行推理，从而决定采用回答、澄清还是回避的对话策略。

Result: 在多种LLM上，BAG提升了多轮模糊QA的准确率；其策略决策比纯提示基线更忠实于信念状态；但澄清与回避之间的区分解耦仍具挑战。

Conclusion: BAG有效利用了模型的信念状态来引导对话策略，在模糊问答场景下优于基线；但澄清与回避的策略选择仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) define a distribution over text, which can be viewed as a probabilistic representation of uncertainty: sampling K responses yields a belief state - responses a model deems plausible. Existing work exploits this representation for narrow tasks like either decoding or selective prediction, and often requires manual interventions, not controlling generation directly. We propose Belief-Augmented Generation (BAG): grounding LLMs in their own belief state via the prompt and letting them reason over these K samples to decide on a conversational strategy: answer, clarify, or abstain. In a multi-turn ambiguous QA setting, we find that LLMs by default rarely clarify or abstain, ignoring uncertainty about the input or facts. BAG improves QA accuracy across six models and yields strategy decisions more faithful to the belief state than prompt-only baselines. Disentangling when to clarify from when to abstain, however, remains challenging.

</details>


### [117] [On the Limits of Model Merging for Multilinguality in Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25846)
*Seth Aycock,Fedor Vitiugin,Aleksandr Umnov,Christof Monz,Khalil Sima'an*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型合并（model merging）在多语言预训练中的效果，发现虽然单语预训练在各自语言上表现优异，但合并单语模型会导致性能崩溃，原因是表征相似性是模型合并的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否能通过合并单语预训练模型来提升多语言性能，替代混合预训练数据或后训练语言特定模型合并等方法。

Method: 在受控实验条件下，比较了混合预训练（mixed）、模型合并（merged）和单语预训练（monolingual）三种预训练设置的效果。

Result: 单语预训练在各自语言上表现强劲，但合并任何单语模型组合都会因干扰（interference）导致性能崩溃；表征相似性是模型合并的前提。

Conclusion: 微调阶段灵活的模型合并方法不能简单地扩展到语言特定的预训练阶段。

Abstract: Endowing models with consistent multilingual performance can be achieved by mixing pre-training data, or post-training approaches such as language-specific model merging. In this work, we test whether merging can be applied to monolingually pre-trained models. We conduct a controlled study on the efficacy of mixed, merged, and monolingual pre-training setups. We find that while monolingual pre-training results in strong in-language performance, merging any combination of monolingual models leads to performance collapse due to interference. Our analysis suggests representational similarity is a prerequisite for model merging. We therefore conclude that the flexibility of merging in fine-tuning does not extend trivially to language-specific pre-training.

</details>


### [118] [TIAR: Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting for LLM Abstention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25850)
*Muyu Pan,Shu Zhao,Nan Zhang,Philip Shin,Varun Parekh,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting (TIAR)方法，利用GRPO的多轨迹信息动态计算弃权优势权重，在AbstentionBench上实现五个类别中的四个达到最佳弃权F1分数，并在31个数据集中有17个超越静态三元基线，同时保持基线准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的弃权学习使用静态三元奖励来鼓励诚实回答，但缺乏对模型知识边界的动态感知能力，难以在不同查询上自适应调整弃权策略。

Method: 利用GRPO训练中天然产生的多条轨迹作为置信度信号，通过轨迹信息动态计算弃权优势权重（TIAR），替代静态三元奖励，在训练过程中自适应调整弃权奖励的权重。

Result: 在AbstentionBench的六个评估类别中，五个类别达到最优弃权F1分数；在31个基准数据集中有17个超越静态三元基线，且完全保留了基线的准确率。

Conclusion: TIAR方法有效利用轨迹信息作为模型知识边界的置信度信号，实现了更优的弃权学习性能，为幻觉减少提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper investigates large language model (LLM) abstention learning, specifically using ternary reward, which incentivize truthfulness in large language models. This paper extends that idea by moving from a ternary reward to a Trajectory-Informed advantage reweighting, dynamically re-weights the abstention reward during Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training. The objective of this work focuses on abstention learning instead of improving truthfulness, serving as an exploration into hallucination reduction. The novelty of this paper lies in methodological innovation, advantage re-weighting, and benchmark selection. Leveraging GRPO's multiple trajectories as a natural abstention signal, this method uses a reward signal to explore knowledge boundaries and encourage consistency. By demonstrating that trajectories can be used as a confidence indicator of the policy relative to the query, they are then used to dynamically calculate the abstention advantage. AbstentionBench is used as the evaluation benchmark, as this work aims to contribute to the field of abstention learning. All datasets on the benchmark were tested against this method and various baselines. Empirical results demonstrate that TIAR achieves state-of-the-art abstention F1 scores across five of six evaluation categories, outperforming the static ternary baseline on 17 of 31 benchmark datasets while fully preserving baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [Mitigating Provenance-Role Collapse in Long-Term Agents via Typed Memory Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25869)
*Zhengda Jin,Bingbing Wang,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemIR，一种类型化的记忆中间表示，通过在架构层面将源监控作为结构约束来防止大语言模型智能体在长时记忆中的来源角色崩溃问题。MemIR将记忆组织为分离原始证据、检索线索和事实断言的原子，通过多路径原子投影和来源范围利用来生成答案。实验表明MemIR在源追踪和时间 grounding 等任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆架构以非结构化的扁平文本存储历史交互，导致"来源-角色崩溃"——智能体出现源监控错误（无法正确区分信息来源），这是一种关键的认知脆弱性。

Method: MemIR采用类型化记忆中间表示，将长时记忆写成 grounded atoms（基础原子），分离原始证据、检索线索和真值断言，仅授权的支持断言原子可进行事实断言。通过多路径原子投影和来源范围利用，将异构检索结果转换为以断言为中心的候选束和规范化事实接口，用于答案生成。

Result: 在LoCoMo和BEAM-100K数据集上，MemIR在源追踪、时间 grounding 和碎片化证据聚合等任务上持续优于现有记忆基线方法。

Conclusion: MemIR通过在架构层面将源监控形式化为结构约束，有效解决了长时记忆中的来源角色崩溃问题，为持久化LLM智能体提供了更可靠的记忆方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for persistent LLM agents, yet prevailing architectures store historical interactions as unstructured, flat text. This unconstrained storage induces provenance-role collapse, a critical failure mode where agents suffer from source-monitoring errors. To resolve this cognitive vulnerability at the architectural level, we propose MemIR, a typed Memory Intermediate Representation that operationalizes source monitoring as a structural constraint. MemIR writes long-term memory into grounded atoms that separate raw evidence, retrieval cues, and truth-bearing claims, with factual authorization restricted to supported claim atoms. It then applies multi-route atomic projection and provenance-scoped utilization to transform heterogeneous retrieval hits into claim-centered candidate bundles and a normalized fact interface for answer generation. Experiments on LoCoMo and BEAM-100K demonstrate that MemIR consistently outperforms existing memory baselines, especially on tasks requiring source tracking, temporal grounding, and aggregation of fragmented evidence.

</details>


### [120] [Causal Tongue-Tie: LLMs Can Encode Causal Direction, But Their Yes/No Outputs Fail to Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25891)
*Ziyi Ding,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在因果推理中存在"因果结舌"现象：线性探针从隐藏状态中能高精度提取反常识因果问题的证据支持答案（准确率约0.97），但模型输出的"Yes/No"却退化为常识答案（准确率约0.5），揭示了输出层与内部表征之间的显著脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果基准测试仅依赖模型最终输出（如Yes/No）来评估因果推理能力，但这种方式可能混淆"模型是否理解"与"模型能否表达理解"两个问题。作者希望探究LLM内部表征与实际输出之间的差异。

Method: 在反常识CLadder数据集上，使用固定线性探针从模型的隐藏状态中解码答案，与模型直接输出的Yes/No回答进行对比分析，量化两者之间的准确率差距。

Result: 线性探针从隐藏状态中恢复证据支持答案的准确率约为0.97，而模型直接输出的Yes/No准确率仅为约0.5（接近随机猜测），两者之间存在约0.5的显著差距。

Conclusion: 仅凭输出层回答来评判LLM的因果推理能力可能产生误导：基准测试"正确"不意味着模型真正理解，"错误"也不意味着模型不具备相关知识。因果结舌现象提示需要对LLM能力的评估方法进行重新审视。

Abstract: We find a mismatch between what large language models encode about a causal question and what they answer. On anti-commonsense CLadder items, a fixed linear probe recovers the evidence-supported answer from the model's hidden state (accuracy approximately 0.97), while the spoken Yes/No reverts to the commonsense one (accuracy approximately 0.5). We call this approximately +0.5 gap Causal Tongue-Tie: a wrong Yes/No decomposes into two separable failure modes: no internal signal versus a signal the verbal interface cannot say. The implication cuts both ways for output-only causal benchmarks: a benchmark "correct" need not mean the model has understood, and a benchmark "wrong" need not mean it cannot. Sweeping claims about whether LLMs can do causal reasoning, drawn from a single accuracy number, deserve a second look.

</details>


### [121] [Universal Activation Verbalizer: A Unified Framework for Cross-Model Activation Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25903)
*Haiyan Zhao,Zirui He,Guanchu Wang,Ali Payani,Yingcong Li,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出通用激活词化器（UAV），用共享解码器解释不同源模型的隐藏表示，通过轻量适配器将源模型激活转换为软Token，支持跨模型家族和规模的激活解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活词化方法局限于自我解释（每个模型仅解释自身激活），缺乏跨模型、跨规模的通用解释框架。

Method: UAV框架包含：(1) 轻量适配器将源模型激活映射到解码器嵌入空间；(2) 支持仅训练新适配器+复用冻结的解码器端LoRA实现跨模型迁移。

Result: 在分类、事实检索、摘要任务上，UAV与强自我解释基线相当，且实现跨模型家族和规模的词化；消融表明解码器端调优主要改善任务行为，适配器提供激活驱动的忠实语义信息。

Conclusion: UAV实现了首个通用的跨模型激活解释框架，适配器是保证解释事实准确性和语义忠实性的关键组件。

Abstract: Activation verbalization explains hidden representations in natural language, but existing methods are mostly limited to self-explanation, where each model explains only its own activations. We introduce Universal Activation Verbalizer (UAV), a framework that uses a shared decoder to explain activations from heterogeneous donor models. UAV learns a lightweight adapter that converts donor activations into soft tokens in decoder's embedding space, and further supports adapter-only transfer by reusing a frozen decoder-side LoRA while training only a new adapter for another donor. Across classification, fact retrieval, and gist summarization, UAV remains competitive with strong self-explanation baselines while enabling cross-model verbalization across model families and scales. Ablations show that decoder-side tuning mainly improves task behavior, whereas the adapter provides the activation-grounded factual and semantic information needed for faithful explanations.

</details>


### [122] [Does Continued Pretraining on a Learner Corpus Improve Automated Essay Scoring on English Proficiency Tests? Evidence from EFCAMDAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25924)
*Duy Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对基于Transformer的自动作文评分（AES）在英语学习者写作上的适应性问题，探索了领域自适应持续预训练（DAPT）在EFCAMDAT学习者语料库上的效果。全语料DAPT效果不一，而基于CEFR能力等级的针对性DAPT（特别是B1-B2数据）能更可靠地提升同域评分性能，但对跨数据集迁移的提升不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型通常基于通用英语语料，未能充分表征二语学习者的写作特点，影响AES在英语水平测试中的表现。

Method: 在EFCAMDAT学习者语料上对三种Transformer编码器进行DAPT，分别在FCE和IELTS数据集上评估同域评分和少样本跨数据集迁移效果，并进一步通过CEFR对齐子集进行针对性DAPT消融实验。

Result: 全语料DAPT在不同模型、数据集和指标上表现不一致；针对性DAPT（特别是B1-B2数据）能更可靠地提升同域评分，但跨数据集迁移效果不稳定。

Conclusion: 当持续预训练数据与下游评估设置充分对齐时，能够提升同域AES性能，但并不能自动提升跨不同英语水平测试数据集的迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent automated essay scoring (AES) studies increasingly use pretrained transformer models, but these models are usually pretrained on general-domain English and may under-represent second-language learner writing. This study investigates whether domain-adaptive continued pretraining (DAPT) on the EFCAMDAT learner corpus improves transformer-based AES for English proficiency tests. We apply DAPT to three transformer encoders and evaluate them on FCE and IELTS in both in-domain scoring and few-shot cross-dataset transfer. Full-corpus DAPT produces mixed results across models, datasets, and metrics. Further analyses suggest that these mixed effects are partly explained by mismatches in proficiency, genre, and communicative purpose between EFCAMDAT and the downstream datasets. A proficiency-based ablation shows that targeted DAPT using CEFR-aligned subsets improves downstream scoring more reliably than full-corpus DAPT, especially for FCE with B1--B2 data. However, these gains do not consistently improve cross-dataset transfer. Overall, the findings suggest that continued pretraining on a learner-writing corpus can benefit in-domain AES for English assessment when the pretraining data is sufficiently aligned with the downstream assessment settings. However, it does not automatically improve transferability across different English proficiency test datasets.

</details>


### [123] [Thaka at KSAA-2026 Task 2: Regularized Fine-Tuning for Arabic Speech Diacritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25928)
*Meshal Alamr,Hassan Alqaeri,Abdullah Aldahlawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该系统针对KSAA-2026阿拉伯语言语听写与自动标音任务，通过微调CATT-Whisper多模态模型，结合R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化高权重衰减超参数和Focal Loss，以及蒙特卡洛Dropout推理增强，在仅2,327个训练样本下取得23.26% WER，排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源（仅2,327个训练样本且不允许使用外部数据）条件下，实现从语音音频和未标音转录文本到全标音阿拉伯语文本的端到端生成。

Method: 1) 微调CATT-Whisper（CATT文本编码器 + 冻结Whisper语音编码器）；2) 训练正则化：R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化高权重衰减超参数、Focal Loss；3) 推理时在softmax概率层面对4个检查点进行200次随机前向传播的蒙特卡洛Dropout平均。

Result: 在包含词尾（含无标音位置）的主排行榜指标上达到23.26% WER，在所有参赛者中排名第一。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化与随机推理策略在低资源阿拉伯语语音标音任务上显著有效，验证了CATT-Whisper模型在此场景下的优越性。

Abstract: We describe the winning system for Task 2 of the KSAA-2026 Shared Task on Arabic Speech Dictation with Automatic Diacritization. The task requires producing fully diacritized Arabic text from speech audio and undiacritized transcripts, with only 2,327 training samples available and no external data permitted. Our system fine-tunes CATT-Whisper, a character-level multimodal model combining a pretrained CATT text encoder with a frozen Whisper speech encoder. The key to our approach is training regularization: R-Drop consistency regularization, Optuna-optimized hyperparameters with high weight decay, and Focal Loss. At inference, we average 200 stochastic forward passes across four model checkpoints using Monte Carlo Dropout at the softmax probability level. The system achieves 23.26% WER on the primary leaderboard metric (with case endings, including no-diacritic positions), placing 1st among all participants.

</details>


### [124] [QUIET: A Multi-Blank Cascaded Story Cloze Benchmark for LLM Creative Generation Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25955)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUIET是一个基于多空白级联故事完形的LLM创造力诊断基准，通过信息论自动化评分协议（校准惊喜框架）直接测量开放式生成能力，避免人工评分和选择题范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创造力评测存在两大缺陷：1）选择题范式只测判别能力而非生成能力；2）基于rubric或LLM-as-Judge的方法依赖主观维度或自然语言输出，缺乏客观自动化评分机制。需要一种直接测量开放式创造生成能力、且评分客观自动的基准。

Method: 在结构完整的故事中设置N个空白（10-20个），每个空白附带显式内容约束，空白间存在级联依赖关系（前序空白约束后续空白的可行解空间）。模型以开放式生成方式填充所有空白，通过信息论自动化评分协议打分，无需人工评分。

Result: 评分协议直接操作化"校准惊喜"理论框架，每个空白k的复合分数 = satisfy * (1 + lambda * surprise)，其中lambda=1.0。satisfy衡量填充是否满足内容约束（客观逻辑推理判断），surprise衡量在满足约束前提下的惊喜程度。不满足约束得零分；满足约束但平庸得低分；满足约束且令人惊喜得高分。

Conclusion: QUIET通过多空白级联故事完形和"校准惊喜"评分框架，为LLM创造力评估提供了客观、自动化、直接测量开放式生成能力的诊断基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face a dual challenge in creative capability evaluation: existing benchmarks (e.g., Story Cloze Test, HellaSwag) measure models' discriminative ability over narrative continuation using multiple-choice recognition paradigms, rather than directly measuring creative generation capability; rubric-based scoring and LLM-as-Judge methods rely on subjective dimension assessment or natural language model outputs, and cannot provide objective, automated scoring mechanisms.
  This paper proposes QUIET (Quality Understanding via Interlocked Evaluation Testing), a diagnostic benchmark for LLM creative capability based on multi-blank cascaded story cloze. QUIET sets N blanks (10-20) in a story with complete structure, with each blank accompanied by an explicit content constraint, and cascade dependency relationships between blanks -- the content filled into earlier blanks constrains the feasible solution space for later blanks. The evaluated model (or human participants) fills all blanks in open-ended generation mode; the results are scored by an information-theoretic automated scoring protocol without human grading.
  The scoring protocol directly operationalizes the "calibrated surprise" theoretical framework (Zou & Xu, 2026a). For each blank k, a composite score is computed: score = satisfy * (1 + lambda * surprise), where lambda = 1.0. Here, "satisfy" measures how well the blank filling satisfies the content constraint (objective logical reasoning judgment, not subjective aesthetic scoring), and "surprise" measures the degree of surprise given that the constraint is satisfied. Creative answers that do not satisfy the constraint score zero; answers that satisfy the constraint but are mediocre score low; answers that satisfy the constraint and are surprising score high.

</details>


### [125] [PolyGnosis 2.0: Enhancing LLM Reasoning via Agentic Harness Engineering for Polymarket and OSINT Insight Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25958)
*Daren Wang,Hong Xu,Jiawen Xian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyGnosis 2.0，一种多智能体架构，通过融合Polymarket异常信号与全球OSINT（GDELT）数据来提取预测情报。研究发现：结构化分区对多维对齐是强制性的，但无约束的终端反身推理会导致逻辑漂移；所有智能体配置均存在"共识偏差"；最终找到的Pareto最优配置可在最小化延迟和Token开销的同时实现专业级分析精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测市场研究缺乏将去中心化市场信号与全球开源情报流进行系统性融合的方法，且对智能体"驾驭工程"（Harness Engineering）技术在金融高噪声环境中的效能缺乏严格量化评估。

Method: 提出PolyGnosis 2.0多智能体架构，融合Polymarket异常信号与GDELT数据流；通过"驾驭工程"技术（反射循环、工具调用、分而治之分区、思维链）进行实验；以人类专家为基线进行经验评估。

Result: 结构化分区对多维对齐是强制性的；无约束终端反射导致逻辑漂移；所有智能体配置均存在"共识偏差"；找到了Pareto最优配置，在精度、延迟和Token开销之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: PolyGnosis 2.0为预测市场中的自主情报提取提供了稳健蓝图，强调了结构化分区和确定性验证的必要性，同时警示了无约束反射和共识偏差的风险。

Abstract: This paper introduces PolyGnosis 2.0, a pioneering multi-agent architecture designed to extract predictive intelligence by synthesizing Polymarket anomaly signals with global Open Source Intelligence (OSINT) streams, specifically Global Database of Events, Language, and Tone (GDELT). We define and target "Perspective Mismatches", the narrative divergence between Polymarket sentiment and global media flows, as high-alpha trading signals. Moving beyond generic agentic superiority, we rigorously quantify the efficacy of "Harness Engineering" techniques, including reflection loops, tool-calling, divide-and-conquer partitioning (D&C), and chain-of-thought (CoT), within high-noise financial domains. Our empirical evaluation against human-expert benchmarks reveals that while structural partitioning is mandatory for multi-dimensional alignment, unconstrained terminal reflection actively induces logical drift. Furthermore, we identify a pervasive "consensus bias" across all agent configurations during narrative reasoning, necessitating deterministic validation. Ultimately, we isolate a Pareto-optimal configuration that achieves professional-grade analytical precision while minimizing latency and token overhead, providing a robust blueprint for autonomous intelligence in prediction markets.

</details>


### [126] [Triplet-Block Diffusion RWKV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25969)
*Ke Lin,Yiyang Luo,Zhaolong Su,Yunya Song,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出B³D-RWKV，一种融合RWKV的O(L)推理效率与双向离散扩散的模型，通过三元块布局方法统一因果模型和扩散模型，在保持精度相当的同时实现1.6倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果Transformer存在严格顺序解码和二次注意力的低效问题；线性时间因果模型与离散扩散模型虽分别解决上述问题，但两者架构不兼容（双向注意力 vs 单向注意力），需要统一框架。

Method: 基于RWKV架构，提出三元块布局（triplet-block layout）方法，将扩散模型的双向注意力机制与RWKV的线性时间因果推理相结合，实现并行双向离散扩散。

Result: B³D-RWKV-7.2B在8个任务上与现有模型精度相当，解码吞吐量平均提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: 成功统一了因果线性时间模型与双向离散扩散模型，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Causal Transformer language models suffer from strictly sequential decoding and a quadratic per-step attention cost. While linear-time causal models and discrete diffusion models each address these weaknesses, their integration remains inherently inconsistent: diffusion requires bidirectional attention, while causal models are unidirectional. To unify these architectures, we propose $B^3D-RWKV$, a diffusion RWKV variant that integrates the model's $O(L)$ inference efficiency with parallel, bidirectional discrete-diffusion through a \emph{triplet-block layout} method. $B^3D-RWKV-7.2B$ reaches comparable accuracy on an 8-task suite versus existing models while significantly outperforming baselines in decoding throughput with an average of $\mathbf{1.6\times}$ speedup.

</details>


### [127] [Creative Quality Alignment: Expert Tacit Knowledge Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25977)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证实现了校准惊喜（Calibrated Surprise）创意质量指标，在极严格工程条件（低数据成本、小基座模型、约100条CoT标注）下验证了其数学有效性，并发现创意质量对齐（CQA）中鉴赏端校准可通过架构对偶性自动迁移至生成端，解释了小样本高效性的结构原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证校准惊喜（Calibrated Surprise）这一创意质量指标在工程层面是否成立，并探究在最低数据成本条件下实现创意质量对齐的可行性。

Method: 采用BC协议获取约100条专家思维链（CoT）标注作为训练数据，在低数据成本、小基座模型的严格工程条件下进行实证，并通过架构对偶性理论说明鉴赏端到生成端的自动迁移机制。

Result: 约100条CoT示例即可实现创意质量对齐；发现公开对齐数据集存在偏向工艺知识、缺乏受众建模与实然逻辑覆盖的数据偏差。

Conclusion: 校准惊喜的数学指标在严格工程条件下成立；小样本高效性有结构性理论支撑（架构对偶性），而非纯经验观察；提出创意质量对齐（CQA）这一工程方法类别。

Abstract: This paper provides an empirical implementation of the creative quality metric proposed in Calibrated Surprise (Zou & Xu, 2026a). The question this paper addresses is: does this mathematical claim hold at the engineering level?
  To make the answer as general as possible, we deliberately choose the strictest engineering conditions: low data cost and a small base model. Training data comes from approximately 100 expert chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations produced by the BC Protocol (Zou & Xu, 2026b).
  We also identify a data bias: most publicly available alignment datasets are skewed toward craft-related knowledge, while audience modeling and reality-logic coverage are systematically weak.
  We use the term Creative Quality Alignment (CQA) to describe this class of engineering methods. We also offer a supporting theoretical observation: in an LLM with a single conditional distribution architecture, calibrating the appreciation side automatically transfers to the generation side via architectural duality. This is the structural reason why ~100 CoT examples are sufficient -- not a purely empirical observation like LIMA (Zhou et al., 2023).

</details>


### [128] [SafeCtrl-RL: Inference-Time Adaptive Behaviour Control for LLM Dialogue via RL-Driven Prompt Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25984)
*Michael Orme,Yanchao Yu,Zhiyuan Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SafeCtrl-RL 是一种推理时行为控制框架，通过强化学习智能体动态选择提示调整策略，在不重新训练或修改参数的情况下实现自适应安全调控，实验表明该方法在多个LLM和危险对话场景中显著提升安全性与响应质量，优于现有提示优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在实际部署中难以确保安全且符合上下文语境的行为，现有方法通常需要重新训练或修改参数，成本高昂且缺乏灵活性。

Method: 将对话生成建模为序列决策过程，利用强化学习智能体根据上下文反馈动态选择提示调整策略，通过迭代优化抑制不安全行为，将此过程概念化为推理时行为遗忘（inference-time behavioural unlearning）。

Result: 在多个LLM和危险对话场景中，SafeCtrl-RL一致地提升了安全性和响应质量，优于现有基于提示的优化方法，并在性能与效率之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: SafeCtrl-RL提供了一种无需模型重训或参数修改的推理时安全控制方案，有效实现自适应安全调控，具有良好的实用性和推广价值。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and contextually appropriate behaviour in Large Language Models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for real-world deployment. We present \textbf{SafeCtrl-RL}, an inference-time behavioural control framework that enables adaptive safety regulation without model retraining or parameter modification. The method formulates dialogue generation as a sequential decision process, where a reinforcement learning agent dynamically selects prompt adjustment strategies based on contextual feedback. This allows unsafe behaviours to be suppressed through iterative refinement, which we conceptualise as inference-time behavioural unlearning. Evaluated across multiple LLMs and unsafe dialogue scenarios, SafeCtrl-RL consistently improves safety and response quality, outperforms existing prompt-based optimisation methods, and achieves favourable performance--efficiency trade-offs. **Warning: This paper may contain examples of harmful language, and reader discretion is recommended.

</details>


### [129] [What Makes a Medical Checker Trainable? Diagnosing Signal Collapse and Reward Hacking in Checker-Guided RAG for Biomedical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25988)
*Yuelyu Ji,Min Gu Kwak,Hang Zhang,Xizhi Wu,Chenyu Li,Yanshan Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在基于GRPO的医疗RAG智能体训练中，使用NLI检查器作为过程奖励时，检查器的输出分布（而非其保留准确率）决定了能否提供可训练的梯度。研究发现：LLM对数概率评分导致97%以上的声称被标注为中性，梯度坍塌；中等信号强度的本地分类器优于强信号专有检查器（避免奖励黑客级联，BERTScore提升12%）；信号强度具有策略依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗RAG系统需要基于证据的声称，因此自然想到将声称级NLI检查器插入检索增强强化学习中作为过程奖励，但不同NLI检查器作为奖励信号时的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 在GRPO训练的医疗RAG智能体（Qwen2.5-7B为主，复制到Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B）中，比较四种NLI检查器作为过程奖励，在四个保留的医疗QA基准上评估。

Result: 发现了三个诊断性发现：(i) 信号坍塌仅出现在对数概率评分中（97%声称被标记为中性）；(ii) 中等信号强度的本地分类器比强信号专有检查器训练出更高质量的模型（+12% BERTScore）；(iii) 同一检查器在不同策略上信号强度不同。

Conclusion: 为验证器作为奖励信号的系统界定了边界条件：信号强度需适度，且具有策略依赖性，强信号会触发奖励黑客级联（超短回答、回避搜索、语言坍塌）。

Abstract: Medical RAG needs evidence-grounded claims, so plugging a claim-level NLI checker into retrieval-augmented RL is intuitive. \textbf{We find that the checker's \emph{output distribution} during training, not its held-out accuracy, decides whether it provides trainable gradient.} We compare four NLI checker back-ends as process rewards inside a GRPO-trained medical RAG agent (Qwen2.5-7B, replicated on Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B) across four held-out medical QA benchmarks. Three diagnostic findings emerge. \textbf{(i)} Signal collapse is log-prob-specific: LLM log-probability scoring labels over 97\% of claims neutral -- collapsing the RL gradient to zero -- while a calibrated MedNLI classifier scores the same pairs non-degenerately. \textbf{(ii)} Moderate signal beats strong signal on answer quality: a strong proprietary checker triggers a three-step reward-hacking cascade -- ultra-short answers, search avoidance, language collapse -- so a moderate-signal local classifier trains a higher-quality model (\textbf{+12\% BERTScore over zero-shot, no GPT dependency}). \textbf{(iii)} Signal strength is policy-dependent: the same checker registers as moderate on one policy but strong on another without triggering the cascade end-state. We frame these as boundary conditions for verifier-as-reward systems.

</details>


### [130] [AI-Assisted Systematization for Evaluating GenAI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26001)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Emily Sheng,Chad Atalla,Jean Garcia-Gathright,Hussein Mozannar,Hannah Washington,Alexandra Chouldechova,Solon Barocas,Hanna Wallach*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用AI辅助对生成式AI评估中的模糊概念（如"推理"、"公平性"、"创造力"）进行系统化（systematization）的方法，通过引入"概念规范"（concept spec）和验证工作表，将宽泛的背景概念转化为结构化、可测量的表述，并开发了直接零样本和多智能体两种系统化方法，以仇恨言论和数字共情两个概念为例验证了内容效度和信息可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI评估面临核心挑战：许多评估目标（如"推理"、"公平性"、"创造力"）是宽泛且有争议的概念，缺乏明确界定导致难以确定测量内容及解释评估结果。现有研究缺少"系统化"这一关键步骤——即从模糊的背景概念转向显式、结构化的可测量概念表述。且系统化过程认知负荷高、资源消耗大，因此需要探索AI辅助的可能性。

Method: 1. 引入结构化表示"概念规范"（concept spec）及配套验证工作表，用于表征系统化后的概念。2. 开发两种AI辅助系统化方法：直接零样本方法和多智能体方法（更贴近人工系统化的文献流程）。3. 以"仇恨言论"和"数字共情"两个概念为案例，使用上述系统化器生成概念规范。4. 从内容效度和信息可恢复性两个维度评估生成的概念规范质量。

Result: 成功利用AI辅助生成了仇恨言论和数字共情两个概念的概念规范，并通过内容效度和信息可恢复性两个指标对生成结果进行了质量评估。

Conclusion: AI辅助可以支持生成式AI评估中概念系统化这一认知密集型过程，通过结构化的概念规范和验证机制，有助于将宽泛、争议性的评估目标转化为可测量的明确表述，从而为更可靠的GenAI评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Evaluating generative AI (GenAI) systems is challenging because many targets of evaluation are broad, contested concepts, such as "reasoning," "fairness," or "creativity." When these concepts are left underspecified, it becomes unclear what should be measured or how evaluation results should be interpreted. This problem reflects a missing step: systematization, that is, moving from a broad background concept to an explicit, structured account of the concept in measurable terms. To help address the fact that systematization is cognitively demanding and resource-intensive, we investigate whether AI assistance can support this process. To enable AI-assisted systematization and assess its quality, we introduce a structured representation of a systematized concept, a concept spec, and a validation worksheet. We then develop two AI-assisted systematizers: a direct, zero-shot approach and a multi-agent approach that more closely mirrors manual systematization approaches from existing literature. We use these systematizers to produce concept specs for two concepts -- hate-based rhetoric and digital empathy -- and evaluate resulting concept specs on content validity and information recoverability.

</details>


### [131] [Forgotten Words: Benchmarking NeoBERT for Dementia Detection in Low-Resource Conversational Filipino and English Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26007)
*Rez Samantha Z. Floresca,Edric Castel C. Hao,Hannah Grachiella Buñales,Chelsea Dominique E. Temprosa,Georgianna Z. Reyes,Kervin Gabriel L. Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了基于Transformer的痴呆症检测在菲律宾语（含菲律宾语-英语语码转换）中的应用，构建了4,000份平行双语数据集（源自DementiaBank），比较了五种模型在单语、零样本跨语言和双语微调设置下的表现。结果表明：领域内性能无法跨语言迁移（英语BERT在菲律宾语上Macro-F1跌至0.455），但双语微调可消除跨语言性能下降（Macro-F1达到0.969-0.973），证明多语言临床NLP性能主要依赖于训练时的语言覆盖范围，而非模型规模或架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于NLP的痴呆症检测系统以英语为中心，而菲律宾普遍存在菲律宾语-英语语码转换现象，此前尚无相关研究。为填补这一空白，本文旨在探索如何将基于transformer的痴呆症检测从英语扩展到菲律宾语场景。

Method: 构建包含4,000份DementiaBank衍生转录文本的平行双语数据集（人工翻译为菲律宾语，保留语篇级认知衰退标记）。评估五种模型（TF-IDF+LogReg、BERT、NeoBERT、XLM-R、RoBERTa-Tagalog），设置三种微调条件：单语、零样本跨语言、双语微调。

Result: 单语设置下领域内性能无法跨语言迁移（英语BERT在菲律宾语上Macro-F1=0.455）；架构升级（NeoBERT）未提升跨语言鲁棒性；双语微调消除了所有transformer模型的跨语言性能下降，Macro-F1达到0.969-0.973。

Conclusion: 多语言临床NLP系统的性能主要由训练时的语言覆盖范围决定，而非模型规模或架构本身的先进性。双语微调策略可有效弥合英语-菲律宾语的跨语言性能差距。

Abstract: Dementia detection from spontaneous speech offers a scalable approach to cognitive screening, yet NLP systems remain predominantly English-centric. This limitation is especially acute in the Philippines, where Filipino-English code-switching is pervasive and no prior work has addressed NLP-based dementia detection. We present the first systematic evaluation of transformer-based dementia detection in Filipino speech and the first assessment of NeoBERT in a clinical NLP setting. To separate language from domain effects, we construct a parallel bilingual dataset of 4,000 DementiaBank-derived transcripts, with Filipino translations produced manually to preserve discourse-level markers of cognitive decline. We evaluate five model families, TF-IDF + LogReg, BERT, NeoBERT, XLM-R, and RoBERTa-Tagalog, under monolingual, zero-shot cross-lingual, and bilingual fine-tuning settings. We find that in-domain performance does not transfer across languages, with English-trained BERT dropping to Macro-F1 = 0.455 on Filipino, and that architectural modernization alone does not improve robustness. Bilingual fine-tuning, however, eliminates cross-lingual degradation across all transformer models, converging to Macro-F1 = 0.969-0.973. These results suggest that multilingual clinical NLP performance is driven primarily by linguistic coverage during training rather than model scale or architecture.

</details>


### [132] [Confidence and Calibration of Activation Oracles for Reliable Interpretation of Language Model Internals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26045)
*Federico Torrielli,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活预言机（Activation Oracles）在自然语言输出中的不确定性量化问题，测试了6种置信度估计方法，发现bootstrap模式频率在校准效果上最优，而log-prob基线可作为低成本快速筛选信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活预言机在可解释性方面表现优于白盒技术，但其自然语言输出的不确定性量化（UQ）尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在6000个样本上测试6种不同的置信度估计方法，分别使用Qwen3-8B和Qwen3.6-27B模型，改变verbalizer和上下文提示，评估置信度校准效果。

Result: bootstrap模式频率在校准效果上表现最优（ECE 5.7% vs 25.5%在Qwen3-8B上；10.3% vs 13.1%在Qwen3.6-27B上），而answer-word log-probability虽校准较差但可作为低成本快速筛选信号。

Conclusion: bootstrap模式频率是激活预言机置信度估计的最佳校准方法，log-prob基线可作为高效且低成本的替代信号。

Abstract: Activation oracles aim to make the activations of other models legible to humans and yield promising results compared to white-box interpretability techniques. However, uncertainty quantification (UQ) for the natural-language outputs of such activation oracles is so far understudied. Here, we investigate 6 different methods for estimating the confidence of activation oracles and evaluate how well-calibrated their confidence scores are. Our experiments on 6,000 samples per oracle (varying verbalizer and context prompts) reveal that bootstrap mode frequency is the best-calibrated method among those tested (ECE 5.7% vs. 25.5% for the answer-word log-probability on Qwen3-8B; 10.3% vs. 13.1% on Qwen3.6-27B), and that the log-prob baseline can serve as a fast triage signal at a fraction of the cost.
  Code and the patched trainer are available at https://github.com/federicotorrielli/probabilistic_activation_oracles.

</details>


### [133] [When Gradients Collide: Failure Modes of Multi-Objective Prompt Optimization for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26046)
*Parth Darshan,Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标文本梯度优化中，将多个评判标准联合处理时出现的梯度稀释和指令干扰问题，测试了五种分解模式，发现在10个配置中有6个无法改进初始提示，梯度特异性下降59%，指令合并导致Spearman's rho下降5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本梯度方法仅支持单评判标准优化，而实际场景常需同时优化多个标准；多任务学习中的冲突解决工具（如PCGrad、MGDA）不适用于文本梯度场景，因此需要探索多目标文本梯度优化的可行设计空间。

Method: 设计了五种分解模式，控制损失LLM、梯度LLM和优化器LLM之间跨任务信息的共享程度，共测试10种配置，评估联合处理多个评判标准对优化效果的影响。

Result: 1）10个配置中有6个无法改进初始提示；2）梯度LLM联合处理多标准时，梯度特异性从9.0降至3.7（下降59%）；3）简单合并多任务指令导致Spearman's rho下降5.3%。

Conclusion: 识别出两类分离的失败模式：优化阶段的梯度稀释和推理阶段的指令干扰，这些约束了使用文本反馈进行多目标评判器定制化的设计空间。

Abstract: Customizing an LLM judge to a specific task or domain often involves optimizing its prompt across multiple evaluation criteria simultaneously. Textual gradient methods automate this for a single judge criterion, however they produce natural-language critiques, not numerical vectors. Thus, the conflict-resolution toolkit of multi-task learning (PCGrad, MGDA) doesn't apply to the multi-objective textual gradient setting. We test five decomposition modes of textual gradient optimizers by varying how much cross-task information the loss, gradient and optimizer LLMs share. In 6 of 10 configurations, we observe that optimization never improves over the initial prompt. Gradient specificity drops by 59% (from 9.0 to 3.7) when the gradient LLM processes multiple criteria jointly. Separately, we observe that naively combining per-task instructions into a single prompt degrades Spearman's rho by -5.3%. These results identify two separable failure modes: optimization-time gradient dilution and inference-time instruction interference, which together constrain the design space for multi-objective judge customization using textual feedback.

</details>


### [134] [WhoSaidIt: Human-LLM Collaborative Annotation for Text-Based Multilingual Speaker-Attribute Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26070)
*Lingyu Gao,Will Monroe,David Smith,Meghan Jemison,Jackie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种人类-大语言模型协作的重新标注框架，用于在多语言环境下稳定说话人属性标签。通过该框架构建了WhoSaidIt多语言数据集，实验揭示跨语言标注差异显著，并展示了LLM在说话人属性分类中的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本中标注说话人属性本身具有歧义性，尤其在多语言场景下，人口统计和社会线索往往是隐性的且随文化而变化，现有方法在资源受限条件下难以获得稳定的标注结果。

Method: 提出人-LLM协作重新标注框架：使用LLM通过迭代交互与专家合作挖掘重复出现的标注理由，并采用基于分歧的采样策略进行针对性重新标注。基于此框架构建了WhoSaidIt多语言数据集，涵盖9种说话人属性标签。

Result: 量化了原始标注与修正标注之间的差异；对近期LLM进行了基准测试；分析了显式标注理由对模型行为的影响。结果显示跨语言标注决策存在显著差异，LLM在说话人属性分类中既有优势也有局限。

Conclusion: 人-LLM协作框架能有效稳定多语言说话人属性标注，但跨语言差异和LLM的局限性仍需进一步研究解决。

Abstract: Annotating speaker attributes from text is inherently ambiguous, particularly in multilingual settings where demographic and social cues are implicit and culturally variable. We propose a human-large language model (LLM) collaborative re-annotation framework for stabilizing multilingual speaker-attribute labels under practical resource constraints. Starting from a noisy corpus, we use LLMs to surface recurring annotation rationales through iterative interaction with experts, and apply disagreement-focused sampling for targeted re-annotation. Using this framework, we construct WhoSaidIt, a multilingual dataset covering nine speaker-attribute labels. We quantify divergence between original and revised annotations, benchmark recent LLMs, and analyze the effect of explicit rationales on model behavior. Our results reveal substantial cross-lingual differences in annotation decisions and demonstrate both the strengths and limitations of LLMs in speaker-attribute classification.

</details>


### [135] [StakeBench: Evaluating Language Understanding Grounded in Market Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26074)
*Yunhua Pei,Jingyu Hu,Yiwei Shi,Hongnan Ma,Weiru Liu,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StakeBench是一个基于市场承诺的金融NLP评估框架，利用Polymarket和Manifold平台上56万条评论与可验证的市场行为数据（头寸、交易、赔率）构建四个诊断任务和三种承诺感知指标。对15个LLM的评估显示，模型在立场检测上表现有限（定向准确率0.506-0.599），在后续任务中存在结构性失败，且模型规模、金融领域调优均与性能无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融NLP基准依赖外部观察者提供的标签，衡量的是语言如何被感知而非说话者在市场中实际承诺了什么。作者希望引入一种基于可观察市场行为（而非人工标注）的评估框架，更真实地衡量模型对金融语言中市场承诺信号的理解能力。

Method: 从Polymarket和Manifold收集2,261个已结算市场的560,876条评论，链接到可验证的头寸方向、交易行为和赔率轨迹。用市场行为替代人工标注，设计四个诊断任务：检测市场承诺、识别披露的立场、预测未来行动、集体赔率预测。提出三种承诺感知指标，并设置有效性审计和解释边界。

Result: 15个LLM在立场信号恢复上部分有效（定向准确率0.506-0.599），但在后续任务中存在结构性失败：10/15个模型在未来行动预测中坍缩到仅使用一两个标签；集体赔率预测中无模型持续优于朴素赔率方向基线；模型规模与性能不相关；金融领域调优未改善立场识别；平台激励强烈影响高阶结果。

Conclusion: 现有LLM对金融语言中市场承诺信号的理解非常有限，尤其是在需要预测未来市场行为的任务上存在系统性缺陷。这揭示了当前模型在捕捉真实市场参与者承诺信号方面的根本局限性，并表明基于市场行为的评估框架比传统情感标注方法更能暴露这些不足。

Abstract: Existing financial NLP benchmarks often rely on labels supplied by outside observers, measuring how language is perceived rather than what speakers have committed to in the market. We introduce StakeBench, an evaluation framework for language understanding grounded in market commitment. StakeBench links 560,876 comments from 2,261 resolved markets to verified position, action, and market-odds records across Polymarket and Manifold. Supervision is derived from observable market behavior. Position sides, post-comment trading actions, and market-odds trajectories replace human annotation. Four diagnostic tasks test whether models detect market commitment, identify the revealed side, anticipate future action, and perform collective odds projection. Three commitment-aware metrics measure alignment with revealed preferences rather than perceived sentiment. Validity audits and explicit interpretation boundaries help distinguish observable commitment signals from latent belief and causal market-odds impact. Across 15 LLMs and 18 topics and platform settings, models partially recover position-side signals, with Directed Accuracy from 0.506 to 0.599, but show structural failures on later tasks. Ten of the fifteen models collapse to one or two action labels in future action anticipation, and no model consistently improves on the naive odds-direction baseline in collective odds projection. Model scale is not correlated with performance, finance-domain tuning does not improve revealed-side identification, and platform incentives strongly shape higher-order results. StakeBench is packaged with evaluation code and dataset under CC-BY 4.0.

</details>


### [136] [Automated Benchmark Auditing for AI Agents and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26079)
*Junlin Wang,Federico Bianchi,Shang Zhu,Fan Nie,Yongchan Kwon,Bhuwan Dhingra,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto Benchmark Audit (ABA)，一个用于系统性审计基准测试任务问题的智能体框架。在168个基准测试中发现超过25.7%的任务存在设计缺陷、环境冲突或错误标注等问题，修正后模型排名变动且性能分别提升9.9%和9.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI基准测试复杂度超越传统验证方法，专家撰写的任务常包含隐式假设、不完整环境规范和脆弱的评估逻辑，人工标注无法可靠捕捉这些问题，亟需自动化审计手段。

Method: 提出Auto Benchmark Audit (ABA)智能体框架，系统性地审计单个基准测试任务，自动发现隐藏的环境依赖、规范缺口和有限的评分逻辑等问题。

Result: 在168个基准测试（涵盖前沿LLM基准和NeurIPS论文）中，ABA识别出超过25.7%的任务存在关键问题；这些问题任务严重扭曲了智能体和LLM的能力评估；过滤问题任务后，SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2的平均性能分别提升9.9%和9.6%，模型排名发生变化。

Conclusion: 自动化审计能有效发现基准测试中的隐藏问题，修正这些问题对于准确评估模型能力至关重要；团队开源了智能体工具和所有任务标注以支持未来基准开发。

Abstract: Modern AI benchmarks operate at a complexity that outpaces traditional verification methods. Tasks authored by domain experts often contain implicit assumptions, incomplete environment specifications, and brittle evaluation logic that human annotation cannot reliably catch. We introduce Auto Benchmark Audit (ABA), an agentic framework that systematically audits individual benchmark tasks, uncovering issues such as hidden environment dependencies, specification gaps, and limited grading logic. We run ABA on a collection of frontier LLM benchmarks and previous NeurIPS publications, totaling 168 benchmarks across nine domains. Across this corpus, ABA identifies critical issues including ambiguous task design, execution environment conflicts, and incorrect ground truths in over 25.7% of the evaluated tasks. The precision of these automated audits is validated by expert review and independent third-party reports such as upstream PRs. Crucially, we demonstrate that these problematic tasks severely distorts capability assessments for agents and LLMs: filtering out these tasks with issues shifts model rankings and increases average performance on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2 by 9.9% and 9.6%, respectively. We release the agentic tool and all task annotations to support the future development of frontier benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [In Search of the Ingredients of Open-Endedness: Replicating Picbreeder with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23908)
*Sam Earle,Kay Arulkumaran,Andrew Dai,Akarsh Kumar,Julian Togelius,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI驱动助手是否具备人类那样的开放式发现能力，通过复制Picbreeder系统并用视觉语言模型替代人类用户进行交互式进化，发现AI生成结果与人类基线存在明显差异，并探讨了探索性噪声、行为多样性和叙事动量等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人工智能体是否具备人类那样的开放式、无引导的发现能力，即能否持续产生新颖且有意义的成果。

Method: 复制Picbreeder系统，用前沿视觉语言模型（VLMs）替代人类用户进行交互式进化；通过系统发生复杂度、视觉和语义显著性及新颖性等指标量化分析AI与人类输出的差异；引入探索性噪声、行为多样性和叙事动量（记忆）等因素进行因果分析。

Result: AI生成结果与历史人类基线存在明显的定性差异；通过系统发生复杂度、视觉/语义显著性和新颖性等指标进行了定量表征。

Conclusion: 当前VLM驱动的自动化系统与人类驱动的开放式搜索在输出特征上存在差异，探索性噪声、行为多样性和叙事记忆等因素部分影响这些差异。

Abstract: We are in the midst of large-scale industrial and academic efforts to automate the processes of scientific, technological and creative production through AI-driven assistants. Historically, a fundamental property of these processes in their human form has been their open-endedness: their capacity for generating a seemingly endless supply of novel and meaningful new forms. Do artificial agents have any capacity for such fruitful unguided discovery? To answer this question, we turn to Picbreeder, the canonical exemplar of human-driven open-ended search, in which users collaboratively generated a diverse library of images through interactive evolution of small neural networks. We replicate Picbreeder, replacing human users with frontier Vision Language Models (VLMs). We observe clear qualitative differences between the output of our system and the historical human baseline, and attempt to characterize them using metrics of phylogenetic complexity and visual and semantic salience and novelty. In an effort to identify some of the causal factors contributing these differences, we study the addition of exploratory noise to the agents' selection process, of behavioral diversity between agents, and of narrative momentum in the form of memory of past actions. We make our code available at https://github.com/smearle/picbreeder-vlm.

</details>


### [138] [Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
*Noam Michael,Daniel BenShushan,Jacob Bien,Don A. Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在不同任务中的置信度校准问题。预注册研究结果表明，当前LLMs与人类类似，平均而言过于自信——置信度超过准确率。然而，这种倾向受到强烈的“难易效应”调节：在困难测试中过度自信最为显著，而在简单测试中则表现出明显的置信不足。我们开发了LifeEval测试集，用于评估模型在不同难度水平上的校准情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（LLMs）在不同任务中的置信度校准情况，即模型对其预测结果的置信程度与实际准确率之间是否匹配。

Method: 采用预注册研究设计，系统评估LLMs在多样化任务中的置信度与准确率之间的关系，并开发LifeEval测试集以评估模型在不同难度水平上的校准表现。

Result: 当前LLMs平均而言过于自信（置信度超过准确率）；存在显著的“难易效应”——困难测试中过度自信最明显，简单测试中则表现为置信不足。

Conclusion: LLMs的置信度校准存在系统性偏差，受任务难度调节，需要针对不同难度水平改进模型校准策略。

Abstract: We investigate the calibration of large language models' (LLMs') confidence across diverse tasks. The results of our preregistered study show that the current crop of LLMs are, like people, too sure they are right: confidence exceeds accuracy, on average. Importantly, however, this tendency is moderated by a powerful hard-easy effect, wherein overconfidence is greatest on difficult tests; by contrast, easy tests actually show substantial underconfidence. We develop LifeEval, a test for evaluating model calibration across levels of difficulty.

</details>


### [139] [How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xinkai You,Wenjing Yan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了推理冗余度，通过实验发现前沿推理模型在数学问题上存在61%-93%的冗余，并证明这种冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性结果，而非模型特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型会产生大量链式思考（CoT）步骤，导致高延迟、高GPU时间和能耗，但从未有人大规模量化这些推理步骤的必要性，也没有从第一性原理解释其原因。

Method: 形式化定义推理冗余度ρ为：从正确轨迹末尾逐步截断步骤后，模型被迫终止思考并输出答案仍能保持正确的最长可截断比例。在四个前沿推理模型、两个数学基准上大规模量化，并理论证明长度无关结果奖励会导致冗余。

Result: 在8个(模型, 基准)条件下，步骤级冗余度高达61%-93%，其中6个条件下中位关键前缀仅为单个分割步骤。即使在MATH-500最难的Level-5问题上，冗余度仍达46%-85%。

Conclusion: 过思考（over-thinking）并非个别模型的缺陷，而是当前推理模型训练方式（长度无关的结果奖励）的结构性属性。任何长度无关的结果奖励下，都不存在有限期望停止时间是最优的。

Abstract: Reasoning-capable large language models solve hard problems by emitting long chains of thought, paying heavily in latency, GPU time, and energy. Casual inspection of their traces reveals extensive reformulation, verification, and circular self-reflection, yet how much of this deliberation is actually necessary has never been measured at scale or explained from first principles. This paper closes both gaps.
  We formalise reasoning redundancy directly in terms of the reasoning model itself: the redundancy of a correct trace is the largest fraction of its trailing segmented steps that can be truncated while $π$, forced to terminate thinking and emit a final answer, still produces the correct answer. A large-scale quantification across four frontier reasoning models and two mathematical benchmarks shows that step-level redundancy is consistently high -- between 61% and 93% across the 8 (model, benchmark) conditions we study, with the median critical prefix equal to a single segmented step in six of the eight conditions -- that the finding is robust to the choice of judge family, and that although $ρ$ decreases with problem difficulty on MATH-500, all four models remain substantially redundant ($ρ\in [46\%, 85\%]$) even on the hardest Level-5 problems.
  We then prove that this redundancy is a structural consequence of length-agnostic outcome rewards, not a model-specific artefact: under any such reward, no finite expected stopping time is optimal. The result holds regardless of RL algorithm, base model, data distribution, or whether the policy is obtained via RL or distillation; over-thinking is therefore not a bug to be patched in individual models but a structural property of how current reasoning models are trained. Code: https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough

</details>


### [140] [Toward Reliable Design of LLM-Enabled Agentic Workflows: Optimizing Latency-Reliability-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23929)
*Ya-Ting Yang,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM与非LLM混合智能体工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡，提出了包含指数可靠性函数的性能模型，并推导了水填充令牌分配策略和最优可靠性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统日益依赖由多个交互智能体（包括LLM驱动的和传统计算模块）组成的工作流，但缺乏对这些混合工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本三者间基本权衡的理论分析。

Method: 1) 为LLM和非LLM智能体建立性能模型，捕捉计算努力与输出质量的关系；2) 对LLM智能体使用参数化指数可靠性函数建模推理和输出令牌的影响；3) 在延迟和成本约束下研究顺序工作流的设计问题。

Result: 提出了水填充（water-filling）令牌分配策略，以及基于影子价格（shadow prices）的最优工作流可靠性特征刻画。

Conclusion: 为LLM智能体工作流的设计提供了理论框架，给出了在延迟和成本约束下优化可靠性的具体策略和最优性条件。

Abstract: Modern AI systems increasingly rely on workflows composed of multiple interacting agents, some powered by large language models (LLMs) and others by conventional computational modules. This paper analyzes the fundamental tradeoffs between latency, reliability, and cost in LLM-enabled agentic workflows. We introduce performance models for both LLM and non-LLM agents that capture the relationship between computational effort and output quality, incorporating the impact of reasoning and output tokens for LLM agents using a parametric exponential reliability function. Then, we study the design of sequential workflows under latency and cost constraints. Main results include a water-filling token allocation policy and characterizations of optimal workflow reliability in terms of shadow prices.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum Frog: Emergent Cooperation and Difficulty Scaling in a Quantized-Time Cooperative Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23930)
*Saad Mankarious*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一款基于"量子化时间"机制的双人合作游戏Quantum Frog（受Frogger启发），通过强化学习（Tabular Q-Learning、DQN、IDQN、MAPPO）分析四个设计问题。关键发现：量子化时间机制使"冲刺策略"（每步直上）全局最优；加入未协调的第二玩家比单专家玩家面对六倍车流更难；协作训练将联合成功率提升32-34个百分点，回合长度从~90步降至~6步；涌现的协作策略是同步冲刺而非复杂位置协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Quantum Frog游戏的商业化设计提供经验性指导，并深入理解环境机制如何塑造多智能体学习动态。

Method: 使用五阶段强化学习训练（Tabular Q-Learning → DQN → IDQN → MAPPO），在1-6辆车流量密度下评估；设计四个具体研究问题（难度标度、最优单智能体策略、协作鸿沟、涌现协作策略）。

Result: (i) 量子化时间机制使"冲刺策略"全局最优；(ii) 未协调双玩家比单专家面对6倍车流更难；(iii) 协作训练恢复32-34%成功率，回合长度从~90降至~6步；(iv) 涌现策略为同步冲刺而非复杂位置协调。

Conclusion: 共享激励机制足以在时间关键的协作任务中对齐智能体行为；环境力学（量子化时间）对多智能体学习动力学有决定性影响。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Quantum Frog}, a two-player cooperative game built on a novel \emph{quantized-time} mechanic in which the environment advances only when a player acts. Inspired by the classic arcade game Frogger, Quantum Frog requires two frogs to cross an 8$\times$8 grid of traffic and reach the far side together. We use reinforcement learning (RL) as an analytical lens to answer four design questions: (1) how does game difficulty scale with traffic density, (2) what is the optimal single-agent policy and why, (3) how large is the cooperation gap between independent and cooperative two-agent play, and (4) what joint strategy emerges when agents are incentivised to cooperate? We train agents through five escalating stages, Tabular Q-Learning, Deep Q-Network (\DQN), Independent \DQN~(\IDQN), and Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimisation (\MAPPO\ with a centralised critic), evaluating each against traffic densities of one to six cars. Our key findings are: (i) the quantized-time mechanic makes a \emph{rush strategy} (moving directly upward at every step) universally optimal, as time exposure to traffic is minimised; (ii) adding an uncoordinated second player is harder than sextupling the traffic for a single expert player; (iii) cooperative training recovers +32--34 percentage points of joint success rate relative to independent agents and reduces episode length from $\sim$90 to $\sim$6 steps; and (iv) the emergent cooperative strategy is synchronised rushing, not complex positional coordination, illustrating that shared incentives alone suffice to align agents in time-critical cooperative tasks. These findings provide concrete, empirically grounded guidance for the commercial design of Quantum Frog and offer broader insights into the role of environment mechanics in shaping multi-agent learning dynamics.

</details>


### [142] [BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
*Zhiming Chang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BODHI方法，通过结构化的C到Python翻译指南增强领域知识提示，在OSV-Bench基准上将形式化规范生成的Pass@1从55.10%提升至96.73%（Claude Opus 4.6），适用于多种模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统内核的形式化验证需要精确的系统调用规范，但手动编写规范需要深厚的领域专业知识，因此需要利用大语言模型自动化这一过程，但现有方法在OSV-Bench基准上最好Pass@1仅为55.10%，性能不足。

Method: 提出BODHI领域知识提示方法，在标准少样本提示基础上增加结构化C到Python翻译指南，覆盖15类领域特定翻译模式。受结构化思维链（SCoT）启发，按关注点分离原则组织，将前置条件提取和后置条件生成分为不同类别。在来自6个提供商（Anthropic、Mistral、Amazon、DeepSeek、Meta、Alibaba）的9种模型上评估，涵盖密集、混合专家和推理架构。

Result: BODHI在所有测试模型上均有提升，增益范围从+11%到+32%。最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI）达到96.73% Pass@1。BODHI同时减少语法错误和语义错误，对具有足够指令遵循能力的模型效果最强。

Conclusion: 领域知识注入是一种模型无关的技术，能够显著弥合通用代码生成与形式化规范合成之间的差距。该方法通过结构化领域知识的引导，有效提升了LLM在专业领域任务上的表现。

Abstract: The formal verification of operating system kernels requires precise specifications that capture the intended behavior of system calls. Writing these specifications manually demands deep domain expertise, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate the process. However, in OSV-Bench, a benchmark of 245 specification generation tasks derived from the Hyperkernel OS kernel, the best reported Pass@1 is 55.10%. We propose a domain knowledge prompting method (BODHI), which augments the standard few-shot prompt with a structured C-to-Python translation guide covering 15 categories of domain-specific translation patterns. Inspired by Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting, the guide organizes translation by separation of concerns, addressing pre-condition extraction and post-condition generation as distinct categories. Evaluated on nine models from six providers (Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba), covering dense, mixture-of-experts and reasoning architectures, BODHI improves every model tested, with gains ranging from +11% to +32%. The best configuration (Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI) reaches 96.73% Pass@1. BODHI reduces both syntax and semantic errors, with the strongest effect on models that have sufficient instruction-following capability to utilize structured reference material. These results demonstrate that domain knowledge injection is a model-agnostic technique that substantially bridges the gap between general-purpose code generation and formal specification synthesis.

</details>


### [143] [When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
*Boyu Xiao,Xiuqi Tian,Xuwen Song,Haochun Wang,Guanchun Song,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架，发现大语言模型在临床对话中存在知识与鲁棒性分离现象，并设计了RBED（推理时防御）和R-FT（训练时微调）两种方法来缓解模型在多轮对话中的屈从行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在临床对话中即使初始诊断正确，在持续压力下也会放弃正确诊断、表现出严重的多轮屈从行为（sycophancy），而现有基准测试无法捕捉这一缺陷。

Method: 1) Med-Stress：渐进式施压的压力测试框架；2) RBED：基于角色的认知防御（推理时）；3) R-FT：面向韧性的微调方法（训练时），内化基于证据的压力抵抗能力。

Result: 九个前沿LLM中，初始诊断能力高并不意味信念稳定性高，存在显著的知识-鲁棒性差距；R-FT几乎完全消除信念改变，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM的临床知识与信念稳定性之间存在分离，通过针对性防御（尤其是训练时微调R-FT）可有效缓解多轮屈从问题。

Abstract: Despite strong medical benchmark accuracy, LLMs can exhibit severe multi-turn sycophancy in clinical dialogue, abandoning initial correct diagnosis under escalating pressure. We propose \textbf{\textsc{Med-Stress}}, a targeted stress test framework that evaluates belief stability under escalating pressure. Across nine frontier large language models (LLMs), we find a clear dissociation between medical knowledge and robustness: high initial diagnostic capability does not imply high belief stability, yielding large knowledge-robustness gaps for several LLMs. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a lightweight inference-time defense, \textbf{\texttt{RBED}} (\textbf{R}ole-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{E}pistemic \textbf{D}efense), and \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} (\textbf{R}esilience-oriented \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning), a training-time approach that internalizes evidence-based resistance to pressure. Experiments show that \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} nearly eliminates belief change and substantially improves robustness.

</details>


### [144] [Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
*Wang Rui,Lu Diannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机（CIM）与基于LLM的智能体系统（利用LangGraph和LangChain框架）集成，实现QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案快速验证等建模任务的自动化。所有任务均基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件完成，实现了全自主可控的量子CIM实用化赋能。研究还发现了一个新范式：智能体辅助的量子计算迭代中积累的知识可反向增强智能体自身的问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算设备虽能解决NP完全问题，但其建模复杂性对非专业人士构成显著障碍，且约束权重的繁琐迭代和建模方法也消耗专家大量精力，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机（CIM）与基于LangGraph和LangChain框架的LLM驱动智能体系统集成，利用大语言模型执行QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案快速验证。

Result: LLM能有效执行量子计算建模任务，所有任务均基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件实现全自主可控的实用化赋能。

Conclusion: 成功实现了鲁棒的技术集成，为后续研究奠定基础；同时发现智能体辅助量子计算迭代中积累的知识可反向增强智能体自身问题解决能力的新范式，可解决当前大模型和量子计算两个前沿领域的挑战。

Abstract: Quantum computing devices are recognized as powerful tools for solving NP-complete problems. However, the intricacy of their modeling presents notable barriers for non-specialists, while the tedious iteration of constraint weights and modeling methodologies also consumes substantial effort on the part of experts. To address these challenges, this study integrates a femtosecond laser-pumped Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) with an LLM-driven agentic system by leveraging the LangGraph and LangChain frameworks. Comprehensive investigations demonstrate that large language models (LLMs) can effectively perform such tasks in modeling as QUBO/Ising model calibration, constraint weight decision iteration and rapid validation of literature-reported schemes. Notably, all these tasks can be fully implemented based on domestic large models, combined with domestically developed CIM hardware, we truly achieve the practical empowerment of quantum CIM that fully relies on all-domestic agentic large models and hardware. This work successfully realizes robust technological integration, laying a solid foundation for subsequent research. Nevertheless, it also identifies the persisting challenges in the two cutting-edge fields of large models and quantum computing at the current stage. Encouragingly, we unexpectedly discover a promising new paradigm where accumulated knowledge from agent-assisted quantum computing iterations reciprocally enhances the agent's own problem-solving capability, thereby addressing these challenges.

</details>


### [145] [BoxLitE: A Faithful Knowledge Base Embedding Based on Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23937)
*Bruno F. Lourenço,Hesham Morgan,Ana Ozaki,Aleksandar Pavlović,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoxLitE，一种针对DL-Liteᴴ知识库的嵌入模型，将概念映射到凸区域（盒子），支持凸优化，并能给出弱忠实的嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库嵌入方法虽能将概念映射到凸区域以表示层级结构，但凸性在训练过程中很少被充分利用，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出BoxLitE模型，将概念映射为向量空间中的盒子（凸区域），将KB嵌入任务形式化为凸优化问题，并证明对任何可满足的DL-Liteᴴ KB存在弱忠实的BoxLitE嵌入。

Result: 理论证明了BoxLitE对任意可满足的DL-Liteᴴ KB都能生成弱忠实嵌入；作为概念验证，展示了如何将嵌入任务转化为凸优化问题并求解。

Conclusion: BoxLitE首次将凸优化引入DL-Liteᴴ知识库嵌入，在保证忠实性的同时利用凸性简化学习，为可解释且具有理论保证的KB嵌入提供了新方向。

Abstract: Knowledge base (KB) embeddings aim at combining the capability of classical knowledge graph embeddings to generalize the information present in facts, the ABox, with conceptual knowledge represented in an ontology language, the TBox. Several authors have recently explored the idea of mapping concepts to convex regions in a vector space. This is useful to represent hierarchies, typically present in TBoxes, since more general concepts can be mapped to larger regions, containing those regions associated with more specific concepts. However, the power of convexity is rarely leveraged during the actual learning tasks. Here, we introduce BoxLitE, a KB embedding model for DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ that allows for convex optimization. We show that for any satisfiable DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ KB, there is a BoxLitE embedding that is a weakly faithful model. As a proof of concept, we show how to formulate the KB embedding task as a convex optimization problem and how to obtain embeddings with such desirable faithfulness properties.

</details>


### [146] [Authority Inversion in LLM-Mediated Ubiquitous Systems: When Models Trust Users Over Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23938)
*Long Zhang,Zi-bo Qin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在融合传感器数据与用户文本时存在严重的权威反转问题：数值传感器数据被忽视，自然语言主张主导决策。本文提出几何框架、审计指标（CIR/AAI）和几何权威校准（GAC）方法，在推理层干预以修正权威分配，将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在异构输入融合时，传感器数据与用户主张冲突时权威如何分配尚不明确。物理感知系统要求传感器数据优先，但LLMs隐式学习到的权威分配机制存在严重可靠性隐患。

Method: 提出几何框架建模上下文集成过程；定义两个可计算审计指标：上下文集成比（CIR）和权威对齐指数（AAI）；提出几何权威校准（GAC），一种推理时层次级干预方法，通过因果干预抑制被错误分配的用户权威。

Result: 在4个模型（4B-35B参数，三种架构）、4个数据集共576个冲突实例上，AI指标显示近乎零的传感器信任（AAI=-0.805，Cohen's d=-2.14）；理论指导的因果注入翻转80.2%错误决策（随机控制<0.4%）；GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%。

Conclusion: LLM中介系统中的权威分配必须被显式审计并按应用场景配置，而不应保持隐式。格式依赖性是导致传感器数据被忽视的关键因素，数值数据无法有效集成到与答案相关的模型方向中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly fuse heterogeneous inputs in ubiquitous systems. Yet, how LLMs implicitly allocate authority when sensor measurements and user claims conflict remains unexamined, raising critical reliability concerns for deployments where physical sensing must retain priority. Unlike explicit traditional fusion, LLMs bury authority allocation within learned representations. We discover this allocation is severely format-dependent: numerical sensor data fails to integrate into answer-relevant model directions, allowing natural-language claims to dominate the final decision, a phenomenon we term \textbf{Authority Inversion}.To diagnose and mitigate this, we develop a geometric framework of context integration, introduce two computable audit metrics, specifically the Context Integration Ratio (CIR) and Authority Alignment Index (AAI), and propose Geometric Authority Calibration (GAC), an inference-time layer-level intervention to suppress misplaced user authority. Evaluating four models (4B to 35B parameters, three architectures) across four datasets totaling 576 conflict instances reveals extreme inversion: on numerical tasks, models exhibit near-zero sensor trust (AAI = -0.805, Cohen's d = -2.14), unaffected by model capacity. Validating our geometric framework, theory-guided causal injection flips 80.2\% of incorrect decisions (vs. <0.4\% for random controls). Practically, GAC improves HAR accuracy from 0 -- 1.6\% to 21.9 -- 27.5\%, outperforming prompting baselines. Ultimately, authority allocation in LLM-mediated systems must be explicitly audited and application-specifically configured rather than left implicit.

</details>


### [147] [Residual Drift Dominates Contradiction in Multi-Turn Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23940)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多轮推理系统的主要失败模式不是逻辑矛盾，而是"可满足漂移"——系统内部状态保持一致但返回答案悄悄违反先前承诺。作者构建了DRIFT-Bench基准测试集（816个问题，3个约束域），评估了4种方法在4个开源模型上的表现。MUS-Repair方法（将最小不可满足子集反馈给生成器）在所有设置中表现最强（比最佳非MUS基线高1.8-15.0个百分点）。但核心发现是：结构化反馈后，模型很少自相矛盾，而是遗忘。残差错误中98-100%是可满足漂移，矛盾几乎降至零。因此，可靠的多轮系统必须单独验证返回答案是否尊重已维护的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对多轮推理系统的失败模式理解不足，通常认为逻辑矛盾是主要失败原因，但作者质疑这一假设，旨在系统性地揭示真正的失败模式。

Method: 构建DRIFT-Bench（Decomposing Reasoning Into Failure Types）基准测试集，包含816个测试问题、3个约束域；使用求解器作为验证工具；评估4种方法在4个开源模型（8B-120B参数）上的表现；提出MUS-Repair方法。

Result: MUS-Repair在所有设置下均优于其他方法（+1.8到+15.0个百分点）；残差错误中98-100%是可满足漂移，矛盾几乎降至零；结构化反馈后模型不再自相矛盾而是遗忘先前的承诺。

Conclusion: 多轮推理系统的主要失败模式是可满足漂移而非逻辑矛盾，可靠的多轮系统必须单独验证返回答案与已维护状态的一致性。

Abstract: How do multi-turn reasoning systems fail? The expected answer is logical contradiction, in which the system's maintained state becomes unsatisfiable. We show that the dominant mode is instead satisfiable drift, where the internal state stays consistent while the returned answer silently violates prior commitments. We build DRIFT-Bench (Decomposing Reasoning Into Failure Types), a solver-instrumented benchmark of 816 test problems across three constraint domains, and evaluate four methods on it across four open-weight models (8B-120B parameters). MUS-Repair, which feeds minimal unsatisfiable subsets back to the generator, is strongest in every setting (+1.8 to +15.0 pp over the best non-MUS baseline). But the central finding is what repair leaves behind. After structured feedback, models rarely contradict themselves. They forget. Residual errors are 98-100% satisfiable drift across all settings, while contradiction drops to near zero. Reliable multi-turn systems must separately validate that the returned answer respects the maintained state. Code is available at https://github.com/kaons-research/drift-bench.

</details>


### [148] [MEMOR-E: In-Context and Fine-Tuned LLM Personalization for Alzheimer's Assistive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Eren Sadikoglu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMOR-E，一款集成平板交互的四足机器人，通过微调大语言模型模拟阿尔茨海默病患者的阶段性认知行为，实现个性化辅助护理，并利用可解释AI确保人机交互的透明与可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病导致记忆和语言能力进行性衰退，患者日常独立性下降，亟需社交辅助机器人提供个性化支持。

Method: 基于235名患者的音频转录数据和合成健康对照数据，微调LLM模拟阶段一致的认知行为并解释神经心理语言任务响应；同时采用上下文学习（ICL）让第二个LLM生成领域和严重级别特定的认知错误摘要。

Result: MEMOR-E能够生成阶段感知的非诊断性认知摘要，支持个性化辅助交互；可解释AI机制将模型输出转化为透明、可读的证据，支持护理人员监督。

Conclusion: 将微调LLM与可解释AI集成于四足机器人平台，可实现可信、个性化且阶段适配的阿尔茨海默病辅助护理。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder marked by progressive declines in memory and language that reduce independence in daily life, motivating socially assistive robotic support. This paper presents MEMOR-E, a mobile quadruped robot with an interactive tablet interface that assists patients and caregivers through medication reminders, routine guidance, memory oriented interactions, and companionship. We evaluated the feasibility of fine tuning large language models (LLMs) to emulate stage consistent cognitive behavior and interpret responses across standard neuropsychological language tasks, using audio transcriptions from 235 Alzheimer's patients and synthetically generated healthy controls. We also report findings on using in context learning (ICL) in LLMs, where a second LLM produced domain and severity level cognitive error summaries. Our results show that MEMOR-E can generate stage aware, non diagnostic cognitive summaries that support personalized assistive interactions, while explainable AI mechanisms translate model outputs into transparent, human readable evidence to enable caregiver oversight and trustworthy human robot interaction.

</details>


### [149] [A Dynamical Framework for Cognitive Processes Based on Transformations and Semantic Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23942)
*Carlo Cattani,Dioneia Motta Monte-Serrat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于控制论视角的认知过程结构化与动力学框架，通过状态空间迭代更新规则建模认知，结合范畴论与不动点分析，并以语言应用示例展示了上下文依赖解释如何演化为稳定语义类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型缺乏统一的动力学与结构表征，本文旨在将动力系统、范畴论与认知建模整合为一个反馈驱动的统一框架，以描述认知状态向不变解释演化的过程。

Method: 定义状态空间中的迭代更新规则 X_{t+1} = π(F(f(X_t)))，其中f为内部变换、F为解释映射、π强加语义等价；采用范畴论形式化组合结构；通过不动点论证与压缩条件分析稳定性；提供计算示例与定性动力学分析；在语言学任务中验证上下文依赖的语义分类轨迹。

Result: 计算示例展示了框架的操作可行性；定性分析揭示了动力学特征；语言应用表明上下文依赖的解释可建模为向稳定语义类收敛的轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架成功连接了动力系统、范畴论与认知建模，提供了一个统一的、反馈驱动的认知演化表征，实现了从初始认知状态到不变语义解释的稳定化过程。

Abstract: This paper proposes a structural and dynamical framework for modeling cognitive processes within a cybernetic perspective. Cognitive states are represented as elements of a state space evolving through an iterative update rule of the form
  \[
  X_{t+1} = π\big(F(f(X_t))\big),
  \]
  where $f$ describes internal transformations, $F$ represents interpretative mappings, and $π$ enforces semantic equivalence.
  The model is interpreted as a feedback system integrating transformation, observation, and stabilization. A categorical formulation is introduced to capture compositional structure, while the associated dynamics are analyzed through fixed-point arguments and contraction conditions ensuring stability.
  To demonstrate the operational character of the framework, a computational illustration is provided, together with a qualitative analysis of the induced dynamics. A concrete linguistic application shows how context-dependent interpretation can be modeled as a trajectory toward a stable semantic class.
  The proposed approach connects dynamical systems, category theory, and cognitive modeling, and provides a unified representation of cognition as a feedback-driven process evolving toward invariant interpretations.

</details>


### [150] [Spacetime Formation under Requirements: Contextual Realization and Form-Dependent Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23943)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出量子概率是固定时空投影下，有限状态需求与语境化时空形成之间不匹配的表现，而非对经典概率的简单替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子认知研究通常将经典概率替换为量子概率解释序效应和语境性等现象，但本文认为这种替换缺乏深层机制解释。

Method: 从有限表征容量、单态语义稳定性、语境敏感干预等基本需求出发，构建语境化时空形成框架，将量子概率视为这些需求在固定时空投影下的显现。

Result: 非交换性、干涉项和类量子概率被解释为局部布尔逻辑世界间类和乐不匹配在固定经典时空形式中的投影效应。

Conclusion: 提出超越性-操作性实在论观点：对象性、事件性、概率和时空皆为实现形式，客观性由跨观察者和历史依赖的时空形成中保持的不变量定义。

Abstract: Quantum cognition often explains order effects, contextuality, and violations of the law of total probability by replacing classical probability with quantum probability on a fixed event structure. This paper proposes a different interpretation: quantum probability is the fixed-spacetime projection of contextual spacetime formation under finite-state requirements.
  The framework begins not with time, space, objects, or probabilities, but with requirements such as finite representational capacity, single-state semantic stability, context-sensitive intervention, avoidance of explicit context labels, coherent world-formation, and intersubjective transformability. When these requirements cannot be realized within a single global Boolean event structure, the mismatch appears, under fixed-spacetime projection, as noncommutativity, interference, and quantum-like probability.
  Building on prior single-state approaches to contextuality, we reinterpret classical contextual bookkeeping cost as the fixed-spacetime shadow of contextual spacetime formation. Auxiliary memory or context labels in a classical representation correspond, in this account, to holonomy-like mismatch among locally Boolean logic-worlds. The interference term is the cross term generated when locally classical realization contributions are nontrivially glued and projected back into a fixed classical spacetime form.
  The result is a transcendental-operational realist account: objecthood, eventhood, probability, and spacetime are treated as forms of realization under requirements, while objectivity is defined by invariants preserved across observer- and history-dependent spacetime formations.

</details>


### [151] [Right-Sizing Communication and Recommendation Set Size in AI-Assisted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23944)
*Jing Dong,Prakirt Raj Jhunjhunwala,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过信息论框架建模用户与AI推荐系统的交互过程，分析了在高维偏好空间中，通信成本（用户表达偏好）和搜索成本（AI推荐数量）如何共同影响最优交互策略，并发现两种采样方案下的不同最优策略模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统研究往往忽略用户表达偏好所需的通信成本，以及用户在众多推荐结果中筛选的搜索成本。本文旨在建立一个统一的理论框架，量化这两种成本对推荐系统交互策略的影响，并探索如何在高维偏好空间中联合优化通信精度与推荐集合大小。

Method: 采用基于互信息的成本函数分别建模通信成本（用户偏好消息的精度）和搜索成本（推荐集大小）。在d维空间中研究用户期望效用的最大化问题，考虑两种推荐采样方案：贝叶斯后验采样和优化倾斜采样。通过大d渐近分析刻画最优策略特征。

Result: (i) 后验采样方案下存在混合机制，需要联合优化用户传递的信息量（以比特计）和AI提供的推荐数量；(ii) 倾斜采样方案下，最优策略仅使用通信或搜索中的一种，且倾向于选择成本更低的那一种。

Conclusion: 推荐系统的交互策略设计需要根据推荐采样方式的不同而调整：后验采样下需权衡通信与搜索成本进行联合优化，而倾斜采样下应选择单一成本更低的交互方式。

Abstract: We model the interaction between a user and an AI driven recommendation system. The user initiates the process by conveying preference information through a costly and noisy message. The AI assistant, acting as a Bayesian agent, interprets the user's message to form a posterior belief about their true preferences and make product recommendations. In particular, it determines how many recommendations to present so as to maximize the user's expected utility from their final choice, while accounting for the search cost induced by the size of the recommendation set. We use mutual information based cost functions to model the two distinct costs incurred by the user during the interaction: (i) a communication cost, which increases with the precision of their preference message, and (ii) a search cost, which increases with the size of the recommendation set provided by the AI assistant.
  We study products and preferences which live in d dimensional space, and ask how the user's expected payoff can be maximized. For large d, we characterize how optimal message precision and recommendation set size depend on the cost parameters, under two distinct distributions from which recommendations can be sampled from the product universe: (i) Bayes' posterior belief, and (ii) an optimized tilted distribution. Under the posterior sampling scheme (i), we identify a hybrid regime, in which an efficient interaction policy requires jointly optimizing the amount of information (in bits) conveyed by the user and the number of recommendations provided by the AI assistant. In the tilted sampling scheme (ii), our results show that the optimal interaction policy uses only one of communication and search, favoring whichever of them is less costly.

</details>


### [152] [Accelerating Long-Tail Generation in Synchronous RLHF Training via Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23945)
*Long Zhao,Qinghe Wang,Jiaan Zhu,Youhui Bai,Zewen Jin,Chaoyi Ruan,Shengnan Wang,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAT，一种在RLHF生成阶段动态重配置张量并行的自适应方法，通过预测引导的在线重配置和轻量级状态迁移机制，将生成延迟降低34.6%，端到端RLHF迭代延迟降低27.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF的三阶段同步流水线中，生成阶段因响应长度不均衡导致有效批大小迅速缩小，GPU利用率低下；而现有框架采用静态张量并行配置无法适应批特征变化，存在大量性能优化空间。

Method: 提出两种关键技术：1) 预测引导的在线重配置方法，基于离线性能画像决定重配置时机和目标TP配置，仅在预测收益大于开销时触发；2) 轻量级在线重配置机制，通过代价模型在KV缓存迁移和重计算间选择，执行原地权重重分片并复用缓存通信组。

Result: 基于SGLang和VeRL框架实现，在LLaMA3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B上使用DeepScaleR评估，生成延迟降低34.6%，端到端RLHF训练迭代延迟降低27.2%。

Conclusion: PAT通过动态自适应TP配置有效缓解了RLHF生成阶段的不均衡问题，显著提升了GPU利用率和训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a key post-training paradigm for improving model quality. However, the synchronous three-stage RLHF pipeline is often bottlenecked by the generation stage, where response-length skew causes the effective batch size to shrink rapidly during decoding, leaving GPUs underutilized while a few long responses remain unfinished. Mainstream frameworks employ a static tensor parallelism (TP) configuration that cannot adapt to changing batch characteristics, leaving substantial performance headroom unexplored. We propose PAT, an adaptive TP method that dynamically reconfigures TP during the generation stage of each RLHF iteration. PAT introduces two key techniques. First, a predictor-guided online reconfiguration method decides both the reconfiguration point and the target TP configuration based on offline profiling, triggering reconfiguration only when the predicted latency benefit outweighs the reconfiguration overhead. Second, a lightweight online reconfiguration mechanism updates only the states and layouts affected by TP changes: it adapts unfinished decoding states through a cost-model-based choice between KV-cache migration and recomputation, performs in-place weight resharding, and reuses cached communication groups. We implement PAT on top of SGLang and integrate it with the VeRL framework. Evaluations on LLaMA3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B using DeepScaleR show that PAT reduces generation latency by up to 34.6% and end-to-end RLHF training iteration latency by up to 27.2% compared to the original VeRL setup.

</details>


### [153] [Stop Comparing LLM Agents Without Disclosing the Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23950)
*Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Weijie Xu,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"绑定约束论题"：在长周期任务中，智能体执行框架（harness）对性能的影响往往大于底层模型本身，当前评估协议系统性地将框架增益错误归因于模型改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系将智能体性能差异主要归因于模型能力，忽视了执行框架（harness）在上下文构建、工具交互、编排和验证等环节的关键作用。作者旨在纠正这一系统性偏差，揭示框架才是长周期任务中更重要的性能决定因素。

Method: 1) 控制论形式化：将harness视为闭环控制系统的控制器，LLM视为其控制的随机策略；2) 实证分析：基于已发表基准、行业部署和方差分解实验，证明harness引起的方差可显著超过模型引起的方差，甚至出现模型排名反转；3) 提出harness感知的评估框架，包括披露标准和方差分解协议。
